QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool Seeds are distributed round-robin across MPI ranks (one GPU per\n//     rank); the globally best seed (max cardinality, smallest index on ties)\n//     is selected with an MPI_MAXLOC all-reduce.\n//   * On each rank, a CUDA kernel grows candidate clusters: one thread block\n//     per seed, the block cooperatively maintains the running \"max distance to\n//     the current members\" for every unclustered point and performs a block\n//     wide arg-min each growth step (same tie-breaking as the scalar code:\n//     smallest index among the minimal values).\n//   * OpenMP is used for the host-side work (compaction of the active point\n//     set, bookkeeping, statistics and validation).\n//\n// The distance is evaluated as sqrt(fma(dx, dx, dy * dy)) on the device,\n// which is the exact operation sequence GCC emits for the scalar reference\n// (-O3 -march=native), so the result bits are identical.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <climits>\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                           \\\n    do {                                                                          \\\n        int err__ = (call);                                                       \\\n        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                               \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %d at %s:%d\\n\", err__, __FILE__, __LINE__);\\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                         \\\n        }                                                                         \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (host, validation only)\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nconstexpr int QT_BLOCK = 256;\nconstexpr int QT_WARPS = QT_BLOCK / 32;\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double dev_distance(double ax, double ay, double bx, double by) {\n    const double dx = ax - bx;\n    const double dy = ay - by;\n    // Same contraction as the host compiler: fma(dx, dx, dy*dy)\n    return sqrt(fma(dx, dx, dy * dy));\n}\n\n// Ordered (value, index) min: smaller value wins, ties -> smaller index\n__device__ __forceinline__ void take_min(double& v, int& i, double ov, int oi) {\n    if (ov < v || (ov == v && oi < i)) {\n        v = ov;\n        i = oi;\n    }\n}\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ void warp_argmin(double& v, int& i) {\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n        const double ov = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, off);\n        const int oi = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, i, off);\n        take_min(v, i, ov, oi);\n    }\n}\n\n// Block-wide arg-min; result broadcast to all threads.\n__device__ __forceinline__ void block_argmin(double& v, int& i, double* s_val, int* s_idx) {\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;\n    const int warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;\n    warp_argmin(v, i);\n    if (lane == 0) {\n        s_val[warp] = v;\n        s_idx[warp] = i;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    if (warp == 0) {\n        double wv = (lane < QT_WARPS) ? s_val[lane] : INFINITY;\n        int wi = (lane < QT_WARPS) ? s_idx[lane] : INT_MAX;\n        warp_argmin(wv, wi);\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            s_val[0] = wv;\n            s_idx[0] = wi;\n        }\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    v = s_val[0];\n    i = s_idx[0];\n    __syncthreads();\n}\n\n// Grow candidate clusters. One block per seed (grid-stride over seeds).\n//   pts      : coordinates of the U currently unclustered points (compact)\n//   seeds    : S compact indices to use as seeds\n//   scratch  : gridDim.x * U doubles (running max distance per candidate)\n//   card     : output cardinality per seed\n//   members  : if RECORD, the member list (compact indices) of seeds[0]\ntemplate <bool RECORD>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(QT_BLOCK)\nqt_candidate_kernel(const double2* __restrict__ pts,\n                    const int U,\n                    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n                    const int S,\n                    const double threshold,\n                    double* __restrict__ scratch,\n                    int* __restrict__ card,\n                    int* __restrict__ members)\n{\n    __shared__ double s_val[QT_WARPS];\n    __shared__ int s_idx[QT_WARPS];\n\n    double* __restrict__ maxd = scratch + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * U;\n    const int with the distance to the seed and\n        // compute each thread's local arg-min (strict '<' with ascending j\n        // gives the smallest index among equal values).\n        double bv = INFINITY;\n        int bi = INT_MAX;\n        for (int j = tid; j < U; j += QT_BLOCK) {\n            double d;\n            if (j == seed) {\n                d = INFINITY;\n            } else {\n                const double2 p = pts[j];\n                d = dev_distance(p.x, p.y, sp.x, sp.y);\n            }\n            maxd[j] = d;\n            if (d < threshold && d < bv) {\n                bv = d;\n                bi = j;\n            }\n        }\n        int count = 1;\n        if (RECORD && tid == 0) members[0] = seed;\n\n        while (true) {\n            block_argmin(bv, bi, s_val, s_idx);\n            if (bi == INT_MAX) break;  // no admissible point left\n            const int best = bi;\n            if (RECORD && tid == 0) members[count] = best;\n            ++count;\n\n            const double2 bp = pts[best];\n            bv = INFINITY;\n            bi = INT_MAX;\n            for (int j = tid; j < U; j += QT_BLOCK) {\n                double d = maxd[j];\n                if (j == best) {\n                    d = INFINITY;\n                } else if (d != INFINITY) {\n                    const double2 p = pts[j];\n                    const double nd = dev_distance(p.x, p.y, bp.x, bp.y);\n                    d = (d < nd) ? nd : d;  // std::max semantics\n                }\n                maxd[j] = d;\n                if (d < threshold && d < bv) {\n                    bv = d;\n                    bi = j;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (tid == 0) card[si] = count;\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device resources held across the outer iterations\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nstruct DeviceContext {\n    int device = 0;\n    int max_blocks = 1;       // resident blocks for the candidate kernel\n    double2* d_pts = nullptr; // N\n    int* d_seeds = nullptr;   // N\n    int* d_card = nullptr;    // N\n    int* d_members = nullptr; // N\n    double* d_scratch = nullptr;\n    size_t scratch_elems = 0;\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n\n    void init(int dev, int N) {\n        device = dev;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&stream));\n\n        cudaDeviceProp prop;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n        int per_sm = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(\n            &per_sm, qt_candidate_kernel<false>, QT_BLOCK, 0));\n        if (per_sm < 1) per_sm = 1;\n        max_blocks = std::max(1, per_sm sizeof(int) * N));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_members, sizeof(int) * N));\n    }\n\n    void ensure_scratch(size_t elems) {\n        if (elems > scratch_elems) {\n            if (d_scratch) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_scratch));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_scratch, sizeof(double) * elems));\n            scratch_elems = elems;\n        }\n    }\n\n    void destroy() {\n        if (d_pts) cudaFree(d_pts);\n        if (d_seeds) cudaFree(d_seeds);\n        if (d_card) cudaFree(d_card);\n        if (d_members) cudaFree(d_members);\n        if (d_scratch) cudaFree(d_scratch);\n        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);\n    }\n};\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA).\n// Every rank returns the identical cluster list.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  DeviceContext& ctx,\n                                  MPI_Comm comm) {\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(comm, &nranks));\n\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);   // always ascending\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices[i] = i;\n\n    std::vector<double2> h_pts(N);\n    std::vector<int> h_seeds(N);\n    std::vector<int> h_card(N);\n    std::vector<int> h_members(N);\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(ctx.device));\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int U = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        // Compact coordinates of the active points (OpenMP)\n        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n        for (int j = 0; j < U; ++j) {\n            const Point& p = points[unclustered_indices[j]];\n            h_pts[j] = make_double2(p.x, p.y);\n        }\n\n        // Round-robin seed distribution over ranks (balances uneven seed cost)\n        int S = 0;\n        for (int j = rank; j < U; j += nranks) h_seeds[S++] = j;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(ctx.d_pts, h_pts.data(), sizeof(double2) * U,\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, ctx.stream));\n\n        int local_best[2] = {-1, INT_MAX};   // (cardinality, compact index)\n        if (S > 0) {\n            const int blocks = std::min(S, ctx.max_blocks);\n            ctx.ensure_scratch(static_cast<size_t>(blocks) * U);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(ctx.d_seeds, h_seeds.data(), sizeof(int) * S,\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, ctx.stream));\n            qt_candidate_kernel<false><<<blocks, QT_BLOCK, 0, ctx.stream>>>(\n                ctx.d_pts, U, ctx.d_seeds, S, threshold, ctx.d_scratch,\n                ctx.d_card, nullptr);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_card.data(), ctx.d_card, sizeof(int) * S,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, ctx.stream));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(ctx.stream));\n\n            // Local winner: max cardinality, smallest compact index on ties\n            // (h_seeds is ascending, so first strict improvement wins).\n            for (int s = 0; s < S; ++s) {\n                if (h_card[s] > local_best[0]) {\n                    local_best[0] = h_card[s];\n                    local_best[1] = h_seeds[s];\n                }\n            }\n        } else {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(ctx.stream));\n        }\n\n        // Global winner: MAXLOC picks the max cardinality and, on ties, the\n        // smallest compact index -> identical choice to the sequential scan.\n        int global_best[2];\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(local_best, global_best, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, comm));\n\n        const int best_card = global_best[0];\n        const int best_compact = global_best[1];\n        if (best_card <= 0 || best_compact == INT_MAX) break;  // cannot happen, safety\n\n        // Re-grow the winning cluster (redundantly on every rank) to obtain\n        // its member list in insertion order. Cost: one seed.\n        {\n            ctx.ensure_scratch(static_cast<size_t>(U));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(ctx.d_seeds, &best_compact, sizeof(int),\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, ctx.stream));\n            qt_candidate_kernel<true><<<1, QT_BLOCK, 0, ctx.stream>>>(\n                ctx.d_pts, U, ctx.d_seeds, 1, threshold, ctx.d_scratch,\n                ctx.d_card, ctx.d_members);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_members.data(), ctx.d_members,\n                                       sizeof(int) * best_card,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, ctx.stream));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(ctx.stream));\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = unclustered_indices[best_compact];\n        cluster.members.resize(best_card);\n        for (int i = 0; i < best_card; ++i) {\n            const int g = unclustered_indices[h_members[i]];\n            cluster.members[i] = g;\n            clustered[g] = 1;\n        }\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list (keeps ascending order)\n        unclustered_indices.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n            unclustered_indices.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    std::vector<double> diameters(clusters.size(), 0.0);\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic)\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        diameters[c] = max_diameter;\n    }\n\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        const double max_diameter = diameters[c];\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(+:clustered_count)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n        \n        printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n               total_clustered, num_points, \n               100.0 * num_points / time_sec;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n               clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n        \n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n            std::vector<double> membershipData;\n            membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n Because\n// sqrt_rn is monotone non-decreasing,\n//   * max_i sqrt(q_i)     == sqrt(max_i q_i)                      (exact)\n//   * sqrt(q) < threshold <=> q < Qt, Qt = min{ q : sqrt(q) >= t } (exact)\n// so the running \"max distance to the members\" can be kept squared, and the\n// expensive FP64 sqrt is only needed to order candidates exactly like the\n// reference, i.e. by the pair (sqrt(q), index). The sqrt is evaluated only\n// when a thread's running minimum improves (candidates are visited in\n// ascending index order, so a larger q can never beat the current best).\n\nconstexpr int QT_BLOCK = 256;\nconstexpr int QT_WARPS = QT_BLOCK / 32;\nconstexpr int QT_COMPACT_EVERY = 32;   // live-set re-compaction period (steps)\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double dev_dist2(double ax, double ay, double bx, double by) {\n    const double dx = ax - bx;\n    const double dy = ay - by;\n    // Same contraction as the host compiler: fma(dx, dx, dy*dy)\n    return fma(dx, dx, dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Ordered (value, index) min: smaller value wins, ties -> smaller index\n__device__ __forceinline__ void take_min(double& v, int& i, double ov, int oi) {\n    if (ov < v || (ov == v && oi < i)) {\n        v = ov;\n        i = oi;\n    }\n}\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ void warp_argmin(double& v, int& i) {\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n        const double ov = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, off);\n        const int oi = __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, i, off);\n        take_min(v, i, ov, oi);\n    }\n}\n\n// Block-wide arg-min; result broadcast to all threads.\n__device__ __forceinline__ void block_argmin(double& v, int& i, double* s_val, int* s_idx) {\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 31;\n    const int warp = threadIdx.x >> 5;\n    warp_argmin(v, i);\n    if (lane == 0) {\n        s_val[warp] = v;\n        s_idx[warp] = i;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    if (warp == 0) {\n        double wv = (lane < QT_WARPS) ? e < live_n; e += QT_BLOCK) {\n                double qv = q[e];\n                const int j = idx[e];\n                if (j == best) {\n                    qv = INFINITY;\n                } else if (qv != INFINITY) {\n                    const double2 p = pts[j];\n                    const double nq = dev_dist2(p.x, p.y, bp.x, bp.y);\n                    qv = (qv < nq) ? nq : qv; Work is complete.\n\n**What changed**\n\nOnly the two existing files were modified: `qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp` and `qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt`. The executable name is unchanged, and the program always runs as MPI + OpenMP + CUDA.\n\n- **MPI**: each outer iteration distributes the candidate seeds round-robin across ranks. The winning seed is chosen by an MPI_MAXLOC all-reduce on (cardinality, index), which reproduces the sequential \"largest cluster, lowest index on ties\" rule exactly. Every rank then regrows the winning cluster locally, so no member lists are communicated. Ranks on a node are spread over the local GPUs.\n- **CUDA**: one thread block grows one candidate cluster. Per seed, the block first compacts to the points within the threshold of the seed, since nothing else can ever join. It then keeps the running max squared distance per candidate in shared memory, with a global-memory fallback when the live set is large, and performs a block-wide arg-min each growth step. Working on squared distances avoids the FP64 sqrt that dominated on these GeForce cards. The threshold boundary and tie ordering are still evaluated bit-exactly against the rounded sqrt, so results are identical to the original.\n- **OpenMP**: host-side compaction, statistics and validation. The OpenMP team size defaults to the node's cores divided by local ranks when OMP_NUM_THREADS is unset, which avoids the oversubscription that had made multi-rank runs slow.\n- **CMake**: the source is compiled as CUDA, with MPI, OpenMP and the CUDA runtime linked. FMA contraction is disabled in nvcc and the distance is written with an explicit fma matching what GCC emits for the original.\n\n**Verification**\n\nCluster membership hashes match the original binary for every case the original could finish, and match an independent same-semantics CPU reference for the larger cases, on 1, 2 and 4 ranks, including a build that forces the global-memory fallback path. Validation passes with `-v`.\n\n| Case | Original | Hybrid, 1 GPU | Hybrid, 4 GPUs |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| N=1000, t=2 | 7.0 s | 14 ms | - |\n| N=3000, t=2 | 478 s | 76 ms | 47 ms |\n| N=10000, t=2 | not feasible | 0.79 s | 0.28 s |\n| N=30000, t=2 | not feasible | 10.0 s | 2.9 s |\n\n**One inherited caveat**: the data generator loops forever for N below 30, because the group size rounds to zero.
